<h3 class="title-foot">ABOUT KIZ10</h3>
<div class="mod-footer-txt">
<p>Welcome to kiz10.com, an inexhaustible source of online fun, a really amazing place! Free games and online free games. Kiz10.com is the best place to enjoy shooting games, war games, fighting games, killing games, Mario games, Barbie games, Dora games, Sonic games, the best arcade games, action games, sport games, flash games, the best puzzle games, online games, free games, strategy games, girl games, cooking games, dress up games and much more.</p>
<p>For all the ages, to enjoy in family. For the little ones and the oldest ones in the house, funny games 24 hours 365 days of the year.</p>
<div style="background:url(//kiz10.com/template/images/marciano_min.png) no-repeat;width:173px;height:260px;float:left;margin:15px;"></div>
<p>We developed our online games and we work with the best developers of the whole world to get the newest games and the last versions of online free games, and because of this we update the site with new free games every day. You can download our online free games, 100%  lawful and secure. We own the 100% rights of them and we offer them freely, to the users and to others webmaster. So don't forget it, kiz10.com is your first source of online free games and the opportunity to download.</p>
<p>If you like shooting games, racing games, funny games, you have found our home, your home, enjoy it as we do it when we work on this every day, not a work but a hobby. The games we develop are thoroughly tested by qualified people, checking until the little details. There are thousands of new people that every day discover and enjoy free games with the best quality, many of them can be compared with games of the best consoles of the market, and kiz10.com makes you easy to find and play the free games we all know and love.</p>
<p>If you think about free and online games, you are thinking about kiz10, where you'll find a huge and new variety collection of the latest online games of the world. Everything here, on kiz10.</p>
<p>Do you like action or adventure games? You have a lot to choose! Or maybe do you prefer strategy games?, motorbike games or car games? ... Terror games or zombie games?, great physic games or platform games... Don't hesitate, enter to kiz10.com and you'll find them.</p>

function createCookie(name,value,days) 
{
	if (days) 
	{
		var date = new Date();
		date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days*24*60*60*1000));
		var expires = "; expires="+date.toGMTString();
	}
	else var expires = "";
	document.cookie = name+"="+value+expires+"; path=/";
}
function readCookie(name) 
{
	var nameEQ = name + "=";
	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
		var c = ca[i];
		while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
		if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length,c.length);
	}
	return null;
}
function eraseCookie(name) 
{
	createCookie(name,"",-1);
}
function TamVentana() 
{
  var Tamanyo = [0, 0];
  if (typeof window.innerWidth != 'undefined')
  {
    Tamanyo = [
        window.innerWidth,
        window.innerHeight
    ];
  }
  else if (typeof document.documentElement != 'undefined'
      && typeof document.documentElement.clientWidth !=
      'undefined' && document.documentElement.clientWidth != 0)
  {
 Tamanyo = [
        document.documentElement.clientWidth,
        document.documentElement.clientHeight
    ];
  }
  else   {
    Tamanyo = [
        document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0].clientWidth,
        document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0].clientHeight
    ];
  }
  return Tamanyo;
}

}

function _updADS(d,f)
{
  var parametros = {"d" :d,"f":f};
  $.ajax(
  {
    data:  parametros,
    url:   '../../template/bin/update.adsvertisement.php',
    type:  'POST',
    success:  function (response) 
    {
      if(response!="")
      { 

          $("."+d).append(response); 
      }
    }
  });
}

function setCookie(cname,cvalue,exdays){var d = new Date();d.setTime(d.getTime()+(exdays*24*60*60*1000));var expires = "expires="+d.toGMTString();document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + "; " + expires;} 
function getCookie(cname){var name = cname + "=";var ca = document.cookie.split(';');for(var i=0; i<ca.length; i++){var c = ca[i].trim();if (c.indexOf(name)==0) return c.substring(name.length,c.length);}return "";} 
var segLoad = 5;

var HttpClient = function() 
{
    this.progress = 0;
    this.send = function(aUrl,parametros,method,aCallback) 
    {
        var anHttpRequest;
        if (window.XMLHttpRequest) 
        {
            anHttpRequest = new XMLHttpRequest();
        }else if (window.ActiveXObject) 
        {
            anHttpRequest = new ActiveXObject("Microsoft.XMLHTTP");
        }
        anHttpRequest.onreadystatechange = function() 
        { 
            if (anHttpRequest.readyState == 4 && anHttpRequest.status == 200)
            {
                aCallback(anHttpRequest.responseText);
            }
        }

        anHttpRequest.open("POST", aUrl, true);
        if(method){ anHttpRequest.setRequestHeader("Content-type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"); }            
        anHttpRequest.send(parametros);
    }
    this.get = function(aUrl, aCallback) 
    {
        var anHttpRequest = new XMLHttpRequest();
        anHttpRequest.onreadystatechange = function() 
        { 
            if (anHttpRequest.readyState === 4) 
            {  
                if (anHttpRequest.status === 200) 
                { 
                     aCallback(anHttpRequest.responseText);
                }else
                {
                     aCallback('ERROR');
                } 
            }    
        }
        anHttpRequest.open( "GET", aUrl, true );            
        anHttpRequest.send( null );
    }

    this.progressFile = function(aUrl,i,o,d, aCallback) 
    {
      var xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();
      xhr.open("GET", aUrl);
      xhr.onprogress = function (e) 
      {
          if (e.lengthComputable) 
          {
           
          }
      }
      xhr.onloadstart = function (e) 
      {
          //console.log("start")
      }
      xhr.onloadend = function (e) 
      {
          aCallback(i,o,d);
      }
      xhr.send();        
    }
}
var _controllApp = new HttpClient();

var objItemMR,_time = '?000001',inMR = 0,totMR = 0,contentMR = '';
function _loadItemMRMINI(i,o,d)
{
    if(i<totMR)
    {
        _controllApp.progressFile('//'+serverName+'/'+o.games.result[i].image,i,o,d, function(response) 
        {
            //console.log("LOADIN FILE ...("+i+")"+'https://'+serverName+'/'+o.games.result[i].image); 

            contentMR += "<li><a href='//"+serverName+"/"+o.games.result[i].url+"' title='Play Game "+o.games.result[i].title+" - kiz10.com'>";
            contentMR += "<img src='//"+serverName+"/"+o.games.result[i].image+"' alt='Play Game "+o.games.result[i].title+" - kiz10.com' width='60' height='60' style='width:60px;height:60px;'></a></li>";

            i++;
            _loadItemMRMINI(i,o,d);
        });
          
      }else
      { 
          console.log("FINISHED DESTACADOS: "+totMR);
          $("#mostrated-mini").html("<ul id='ul-list-minimr'>"+contentMR+"<ul>");
      }
}
function _loadMRMINI()
{
    _controllApp.get('../cache/pages/index/_rated.json'+_time, function(response) 
    {
      if(response!="ERROR")
      {
          objItemMR = JSON.parse(response);
          totMR = parseInt(objItemMR.games.items);
          _loadItemMRMINI(inMR,objItemMR,contentMR);
      }
    }); 

}
if(parseInt($(window).width())>800){ _loadMRMINI(); }

{
	$("body").css("overflow", "auto");
	var WINDOW_ELE = getWindowData();
	var Y = (((WINDOW_ELE[1]/2)-160) / 2);
	var posTOP = (((WINDOW_ELE[1]/2)-160) + (Y - (700/2)));	
	$("#albx-msg,#albx-new,#albx-not").fadeOut();
	$("#cnt-formmsg").html("");	
	$("#bg-preload-box").fadeIn(100);
	$("#cnt-message-box").fadeIn(1000);
	$("#cnt-form-message").width(700);$("#cnt-form-message").height(50);
	$("#cnt-form-message").animate({marginTop: posTOP},
	{
		duration: 500,
		complete: function() 
		{ 
			$("#cnt-form-message").animate({ height: 350},
			{
			duration: 200,complete: function(){ loadFREEGAMES(L); } 
			});
		}
	});
}
function loadFREEGAMES(L)
{
	var parametros = {"PROCESS":"FREGAMES"};
	$.ajax(
	{
		data:  parametros,
		url:   '../../template/bin/load.freegames.php',
		type:  'POST',
		beforeSend: function () 
		{	
			$("#cnt-formmsg").html("<div style='float:left;width:100%;margin:170px 0px 0px 0px;height:15px;background:url(//kiz10.com/template/images/loading-points.gif) no-repeat center center;'></div>");
		},
		success:  function (response) 
		{
			if(L=='')
			{
				$("#cnt-form-message").height(510);$("#cnt-formmsg").html(response);
			}else
			{
				var i=0;var clockResponse;
				clockResponse = setInterval(function (){ if(i==2){ clearTimeout(clockResponse);$("#cnt-form-message").height(510);$("#cnt-formmsg").html(response); }else{i++;} },500);
			}
		}
	});
}
function closeAlert(){ $("#box-alert-user").fadeOut(500); }
	


function getWindowData(){ 
    var widthViewport,heightViewport,xScroll,yScroll,widthTotal,heightTotal; 
    if (typeof window.innerWidth != 'undefined'){ 
        widthViewport= window.innerWidth-17; 
        heightViewport= window.innerHeight-17; 
    }else if(typeof document.documentElement != 'undefined' && typeof document.documentElement.clientWidth !='undefined' && document.documentElement.clientWidth != 0){ 
        widthViewport=document.documentElement.clientWidth; 
        heightViewport=document.documentElement.clientHeight; 
    }else{ 
        widthViewport= document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0].clientWidth; 
        heightViewport=document.getElementsByTagName('body')[0].clientHeight; 
    } 
    xScroll=self.pageXOffset || (document.documentElement.scrollLeft+document.body.scrollLeft);
    yScroll=self.pageYOffset || (document.documentElement.scrollTop+document.body.scrollTop);
    widthTotal=Math.max(document.documentElement.scrollWidth,document.body.scrollWidth,widthViewport);
    heightTotal=Math.max(document.documentElement.scrollHeight,document.body.scrollHeight,heightViewport);
    return [widthViewport,heightViewport,xScroll,yScroll,widthTotal,heightTotal]; 
}

}

function renderProgress(progress)
{	
	//console.log("Progress : "+progress);
	if(progress<=100 && progress>0)
	{	
		progress = Math.floor(progress);
		if(progress<25){
			var angle = -90 + (progress/100)*360;
			$(".animate-0-25-b").css("transform","rotate("+angle+"deg)");
		}
		else if(progress>=25 && progress<50){
			var angle = -90 + ((progress-25)/100)*360;
			$(".animate-0-25-b").css("transform","rotate(0deg)");
			$(".animate-25-50-b").css("transform","rotate("+angle+"deg)");
		}
		else if(progress>=50 && progress<75){
			var angle = -90 + ((progress-50)/100)*360;
			$(".animate-25-50-b, .animate-0-25-b").css("transform","rotate(0deg)");
			$(".animate-50-75-b").css("transform","rotate("+angle+"deg)");
		}
		else if(progress>=75 && progress<=100){
			var angle = -90 + ((progress-75)/100)*360;
			$(".animate-50-75-b, .animate-25-50-b, .animate-0-25-b")
															  .css("transform","rotate(0deg)");
			$(".animate-75-100-b").css("transform","rotate("+angle+"deg)");
		}
		$(".txt-progress").html(progress+"%");
	}
}

function clearProgress(){ $(".animate-75-100-b, .animate-50-75-b, .animate-25-50-b, .animate-0-25-b").css("transform","rotate(90deg)"); }


function getbrowserwidth()
{
	if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf("MSIE") > 0)
	{
		return(document.body.clientWidth);
	}
	else
	{
		return window.outerWidth;
	}
}
function getbrowserheight()
{
	if (navigator.userAgent.indexOf("MSIE") > 0)
	{
		return(document.body.clientHeight);
	}
	else
	{
		return(window.outerHeight);
	}
}
var popup = new Object()
function centrar_popup(URL, width, height)
{
	var X = getbrowserwidth() / 2
	var Y = getbrowserheight() / 2
	popup = window.open(URL, 'PopUp','scrollbars=yes ' +'width=' + width + ' ' +'height=' + height + ' ' +'top=' + (window.screenTop + (Y - (height/2))) + ' ' +'left=' + (window.screenLeft + (X - (width/2)))
	)
	popup.focus()
}
function LoginTwitter()
{
	var url = "//kiz10.com/applications/social/twitter/login.php";
	var ancho = 700,alto = 650;
	centrar_popup(url,ancho,alto);			
}

function openBoxterm(title,file){var type = "zoomin",width="100%",height="450";if(file=='mincontact'){height="260";}$('#titleTerms').html(title);$('.overlay-container').fadeIn(function() {$('#box-terms').css('display','block');$('<IFRAME FRAMEBORDER=0 MARGINWIDTH=0 MARGINHEIGHT=0 OVERFLOW-Y="scroll" OVERFLOW-x="hidden" WIDTH='+width+' HEIGHT='+height+' SRC="//kiz10.com/template/'+file+'.php?title="></IFRAME>').appendTo('#box-terms');window.setTimeout(function(){$('.window-container.'+type).addClass('window-container-visible');$('.down-window').addClass('window-container-visible');}, 100);});}

function loadCSS(FILENAME)
{
	var fileref = document.createElement("link");
	fileref.setAttribute("rel", "stylesheet");
	fileref.setAttribute("type", "text/css");
	fileref.setAttribute("href",FILENAME);

	if (typeof fileref != "undefined")
	{
	   document.getElementsByTagName("head")[0].appendChild(fileref);
	}
}

function _createModalForm()
{
	var cnt = '';
	cnt += '<div class="window-container zoomin" id="modal-recomend-game">';
	cnt += '	<h3 id="titleTerms"></h3>';
	cnt += '	<div id="box-terms"></div>';
	cnt += '	<span class="close">Close</span>';
	cnt += '	<a rel="nofollow" href="javascript:;" id="btnClose" onClick="closeModal();"><img src="//kiz10.com/template/images/icon_close.png" width="28px" height="31px"></a>';
	cnt += '</div>';
	$('#modl-modalform').prepend(cnt);
}
function closeModal()
{
	$('.overlay-container').fadeOut().end().find('.window-container').removeClass('window-container-visible');
	$('.down-window').removeClass('window-container-visible');
	$('#box-terms').empty();
	$('#box-terms').css('display','none');
}

function _obligate()
{
	var i=0,clockResponse;
	clockResponse = setInterval(function ()
	{ 
		if(i==6)
		{ 
			clearTimeout(clockResponse);
			if($("#preloader-kiz").length){ show_games();$("#preloader-kiz").remove(); } 
		}else{i++;} 
	},1000);
}

function submitSearch()
{
	var q = document.getElementById("q").value;
	if(q!="")
	{
		document.getElementById("search").value = q;
		var search = q;
		search = search.toLowerCase();
		var n = search.length;
		var frm = document.getElementById('searchform') || null;
		if(frm && n>=2) 
		{ 
			frm.action = "//"+serverName+"/search/";
			if(search!="Search games or users"){ frm.submit(); }   
		}
	}
}

var face_api_id = _FACEBOOK_API;
var url_actual = _REQUEST_URI;
var face_getuser = _FACEBOOK_USER;
window.fbAsyncInit = function()
{
	FB.init
	({
		  appId      : face_api_id, // Set YOUR APP ID
		  status     : true, // check login status
		  cookie     : true, // enable cookies to allow the server to access the session
		  xfbml      : true  // parse XFBML
	});
	FB.Event.subscribe('auth.authResponseChange', function(response) { }); 
};

function Login()
{
    FB.login(function(response) 
	{
        if (response.authResponse) 
        {
			if(url_actual=='/login' || url_actual=='/register')
			{
			document.getElementById("box-loading").style.display = "block";
			}
            getUserInfo();
        } else 
        {
			console.log('User cancelled login or did not fully authorize.');
        }
    },{scope: 'public_profile,email,user_friends'});
}	
function getUserInfo() 
{	
	FB.api('/me',function(response)
	{
		_loginNet = 'FACEBOOK';	

		if(_REQUESTURI!='/login' && _REQUESTURI!='/register')
		{
			_accessNetwork(response);
		}else{
			LoginNewtWork(response);
		}
	});
}
function Logout()
{	
	if(face_getuser!="")
	{
        FB.logout(function(){location.href = "//kiz10.com/logout";});
	}else{
		location.href = "//kiz10.com/logout";
	}
}
// Load the SDK asynchronously
(function(d){
     var js, id = 'facebook-jssdk', ref = d.getElementsByTagName('script')[0];
     if (d.getElementById(id)) {return;}
     js = d.createElement('script'); 
	 js.id = id; 
	 js.async = true;
     js.src = "//connect.facebook.net/en_US/all.js";
     ref.parentNode.insertBefore(js, ref);
}(document));

	Number.prototype.numberFormat = function(decimals, dec_point, thousands_sep) 
	{
		dec_point = typeof dec_point !== 'undefined' ? dec_point : '.';
		thousands_sep = typeof thousands_sep !== 'undefined' ? thousands_sep : ',';

		var parts = this.toFixed(decimals).split('.');
		parts[0] = parts[0].replace(/\B(?=(\d{3})+(?!\d))/g, thousands_sep);

		return parts.join(dec_point);
	}

	function _getFacebookLikes()
	{	
		var pagina = "//www.facebook.com/kiz10";
		var url = "https://graph.facebook.com/fql?q=SELECT%20url,%20normalized_url,%20share_count,%20like_count,%20comment_count,%20total_count,commentsbox_count,%20comments_fbid,%20click_count%20FROM%20link_stat%20WHERE%20url=%27"+pagina+"%27";
		var count = 0;
		$.getJSON(url,function(json)
		{
			count = json.data[0].total_count;
			$('#likes-kiz10').html(count.numberFormat(0, ',', '.'));
		});
	}
	//_getFacebookLikes();
